PSED v2 ontology

Written by psed_v2/ontology/grow.py (report / apply) from ontology.yaml, units.yaml, history.csv, rounds.csv, pending.csv, never.csv and unmapped.csv. Papers 1-12 (the pilot golds) are development papers (P157f); held-out papers (13+) are refused by grow.py add.

1. Kinds

quantity:    something with a number: measured, set, fitted or computed; carries a unit (or "1" for dimensionless). Example: film_thickness, deposition_temperature.
categorical: a field whose value is a name from a fixed list, not a number. Example: material, carrier_gas, crystallinity.
species:     a chemical thing: element, molecule, ion or radical, named as the paper names it. Example: tma, h2o, o_atom.
structure:   a physical object or shape the deposition acts on or produces: substrate, trench, pillar array, film, porous body. Example: pillarhall_4.
mechanism:   a physical or chemical process the paper invokes to explain a result, not a number. Example: gas_diffusion, surface_recombination.
method:      how something was deposited, measured or computed; a measurement method is what makes a measurement activity. Example: spectroscopic_ellipsometry, xps.
model:       a named mathematical or simulation model the paper fits or runs; its parameters are quantities with role model_parameter. Example: ylilammi_diffusion_reaction, gordon_plug_flow.

Not entries: units live in units.yaml (symbol, spelling variants, dimension, factor); qualifiers are the 26 keys that narrow a fact (step, stage, stat, …) under qualifiers:.

6. Families

familymemberswhat they answer
compositionelement_fraction (at.%; element); element_ratio (1; element, reference_element); phase_fraction (wt%; phase, relative_to)element_fraction: The atomic fraction of an element among all atoms of the film counted by the stated method, in at | element_ratio: Atoms of an element per atom of a reference element in the film, as the stated method sees it (mat22's S/Mo stoichiometry x in MoSx; H per metal atom), a unitless ratio (Sec | phase_fraction: The weight fraction of a named phase (the phase qualifier) among the phases the stated method sees, in wt%; by XRD Rietveld refinement only the crystalline part is counted (relative_to crystalline_phases), which gre23 does not state (not_stated) (Sec
conformalitycoated_aspect_ratio (1; basis); penetration_depth (µm; level); step_coverage (1; position); normalized_thickness (1; reference); fill_fraction (%; )coated_aspect_ratio: The (equivalent) aspect ratio reached by the coating: equal to that of the structure for complete coverage, otherwise calculated from PD50% (Cremers 2019, Sec | penetration_depth: The depth at which the film thickness equals a given fraction of the film thickness at the top: 50% for PD50%, 80% for PD80% (Cremers 2019, Sec | step_coverage: The ratio of the film thickness at the bottom of a feature (or at the side wall) to the film thickness at the top of the feature (Cremers 2019, Sec | normalized_thickness: Film thickness divided by a reference thickness: the thickness at the feature entrance, on the planar surface, or an assumed saturated thickness | fill_fraction: The share of a channel's height filled by the film on its two walls at the end of a run (yim20: 2 S_IIb / H)
growth_amountmass_gain (%; relative_to); moles_deposited (mmol; ); moles_from_mass_gain (mmol; ); deposited_moles_per_cycle (µmol; ); atoms_per_cycle (nm-2; element, over); coating_mass_fraction (%; ); shell_volume_fraction (%; )mass_gain: Mass gained by a specimen in a deposition, relative to its initial or final mass (the relative_to qualifier), as a percentage | moles_deposited: Moles of film material deposited in one run, from the deposited moles per cycle times the cycle number (a transform; gre23 mass balance) | moles_from_mass_gain: Moles of film material deposited in one run from the gravimetric mass gain and the material's molar mass (a transform; the second route of gre23's mass balance) | deposited_moles_per_cycle: Moles of solid deposited per ALD cycle, from the pressure trace of the reactant half-reaction (gre23 Δn_s, Eq | atoms_per_cycle: Metal atoms deposited per unit area per cycle, the amount-based growth per cycle (Cremers 2019 Sec | coating_mass_fraction: The coating's share of the coated specimen's mass: mass gain / (1 + mass gain) with the gain relative to the initial mass (a transform; not a phase fraction) | shell_volume_fraction: The coating's share of a porous compact's volume for spherical particles of the stated diameter and coating thickness (a transform)

7. Structure classes

  • structure: Any substrate or test structure on which conformality is quantified. [Cremers 2019 Sec. III, Table I 'Structure']
    slots: none
    • planar: A flat substrate without a 3D feature; it carries the planar reference values, conformality is not quantified on it. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II H (the planar reference thickness)]
      slots: none
      instances: planar
    • har_feature: A high-aspect-ratio feature with an opening of width w and a depth L along which the reactant diffuses; aspect ratio L/w, third dimension z. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II G, Fig. 5 (w, L, z; AR = L/w, Eq. 11)]
      slots: feature_depth, feature_width, feature_extent, aspect_ratio
      • trench: A vertical trench etched into a substrate: width w, depth L, length z. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II G, Fig. 5 (the width of a trench)]
        slots: feature_depth, feature_width, feature_extent, aspect_ratio
        instances: si_trench_capped
      • via_hole: A vertical hole: diameter w, depth L. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II G, Fig. 5 (the diameter of a hole)]
        slots: feature_depth, feature_width, aspect_ratio
        instances: etched_hole_array
      • lateral_channel: A lateral high-aspect-ratio channel (LHAR) under a roof: channel height w, lateral length L along the diffusion (the channel length, feature_depth; the former mechanism channel_length is superseded by it, P82), channel width z. pillarhall_3 and other LHAR designs are instances. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II G, Fig. 5 (channel height w, channel length L of a lateral structure); Sec. III]
        slots: feature_depth, feature_width, feature_extent, aspect_ratio
        instances: pillarhall_3, macroscopic_lateral_trench, pillarhall_1, pillarhall_4
    • porous_body: A body the reactant enters through a continuous pore network from one or two faces: full thickness 2L (body_thickness), entry sides, diffusion length L, pore diameter w as the smallest opening, particle diameter, porosity, specific surface area, lateral size. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II G ('porous substrates': the pore size as w); gre23 Sec. II A (s6.p8, s8.p1)]
      slots: body_thickness, entry_sides, feature_depth, feature_width, particle_diameter, porosity, specific_surface_area, body_diameter, aspect_ratio
      instances: porous_sphere, porous_slab, porous_cylinder
      • nanoparticle_compact: A pressed compact of nanoparticles; the pores are the gaps between the particles, their diameter about the particle diameter. [gre23 Sec. II A (Fig. 1, s8.p1); Cremers 2019 Sec. II G]
        slots: body_thickness, entry_sides, feature_depth, feature_width, particle_diameter, porosity, specific_surface_area, body_diameter, aspect_ratio
        instances: nanoparticle_compact
      • aerogel_monolith: A monolithic aerogel, porosity above 90 %, the highest aspect ratios coated by ALD. [gre23 Sec. II A (s6.p8: aerogel monoliths of higher AR than the compacts); Cremers 2019 Sec. II G]
        slots: body_thickness, entry_sides, feature_depth, feature_width, particle_diameter, porosity, specific_surface_area, body_diameter, aspect_ratio
      • nanopore_membrane: An ordered array of straight pores through a membrane (anodic alumina and similar). [Cremers 2019 Sec. III, Table I (anodic aluminium oxide membranes as a structure to quantify conformality)]
        slots: body_thickness, entry_sides, feature_depth, feature_width, particle_diameter, porosity, specific_surface_area, body_diameter, aspect_ratio
        instances: anodic_alumina_membrane

8. Hierarchy

  • ald_process holds 4 (categorical 4): What is deposited and from what: the film material, its reactants A and B, and the process type (thermal, ozone-based, plasma-enhanced). Popov counts a process as a material plus its reactant combination; Cremers tabulates an experiment under 'Film (process)', e.g. Al2O3 (TMA/H2O). [Popov 2025 Sec. II A ('1711 unique thermal ALD processes for 539 thin film materials') and Table I columns Material, Reactant A, Reactant B; Cremers 2019 Sec. IV ('We distinguish three types of ALD processes: thermal, ozone-based, and plasma-enhanced') and Tables II-IV column 'Film (process)']
    ids: process_mode, material, reactant_A, reactant_B
  • process_parameters holds 17 (quantity 16, categorical 1): Settings chosen by the experimenter for a deposition, Cremers' own term: the settings of each step of the ALD cycle and the settings of the whole run. [Cremers 2019 Sec. III B ('The process parameters were identical for both substrates: T = 100 C, P_TMA = 0.28 Pa, and P_H2O = 0.25 Pa. During the process, 1000 ALD cycles were applied with the following pulse/pump times'); Sec. IV ('the process parameters were not always reported')]
    • cycle_step holds 8 (quantity 8): Settings of one step of the ALD cycle: pulse and purge times, the reactant partial pressure, their product the exposure, the plasma power of a plasma step, and the time within a reactant pulse along which a trace is recorded. [Cremers 2019 Sec. I (the four steps of the ALD cycle), Sec. II D ('the product of the reactant partial pressure and the pulse time'), Sec. II E Eq. 9 (d theta / dt within a pulse), Sec. IV C ('the gas pressure, the RF power, the plasma exposure time')]
      ids: pulse_time, purge_time, partial_pressure, exposure, plasma_power, time, plasma_gas_ratio, cycle_time
    • deposition_run holds 9 (categorical 1, quantity 8): Settings that hold for the whole deposition run: deposition temperature, reactor pressure, number of cycles, carrier gas and its flow, and precursor source temperature. [Cremers 2019 Sec. III B ('T = 100 C', '1000 ALD cycles were applied', 'in the same ALD run'), Tables II-IV column 'T (C)', Sec. II C (reactants entrained in an inert carrier gas); Popov 2025 Sec. IV ('source temperature')]
      ids: carrier_gas, deposition_temperature, process_pressure, source_temperature, cycle_number, gas_flow_rate, precursor_purity, annealing_temperature, wall_temperature
  • ald_reactor holds 5 (categorical 4, quantity 1): Properties of the equipment: the reactor and its manufacturer, its class by working pressure, how reactants are delivered, and the configuration of the plasma source. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II C 'ALD reactor types' (pump-type, flow-type, AP-type; 'can easily be adapted to accommodate plasma sources'), Table IV ('plasma configuration'); schema-miner ProcessParameters/Reactor and DeliveryMethod]
    ids: reactor, delivery_method, reactor_type, plasma_configuration, base_pressure
  • test_structure holds 22 (categorical 4, quantity 18): The substrate or test structure on which conformality is quantified, with its geometry (width w, depth L, extent z, aspect ratio, equivalent aspect ratio) and the position inside it along which a profile is recorded (distance, distance over width). [Cremers 2019 Sec. III 'Structures to quantify the conformality of ALD' and Table I; Sec. II G (AR = L/w, Eq. 11; EAR); Tables II-IV columns 'Substrate' and 'EAR'; Sec. IV ('film thickness as a function of the depth of the structure')]
    ids: structure, aspect_ratio, equivalent_aspect_ratio, feature_width, feature_depth, feature_extent, distance, dimensionless_distance, substrate, porosity, pore_density, tortuosity, pore_volume, pillar_layout, body_thickness, entry_sides, particle_diameter, specific_surface_area, body_diameter, knudsen_diffusion_coefficient, effective_diffusion_coefficient, gas_phase_diffusion_coefficient
  • deposited_film holds 35 (quantity 33, categorical 2): What is observed on the sample after deposition: the film growth, its conformality inside 3D features, and the properties of the film material. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II A (uniform films along a planar substrate, conformal films inside 3D features); schema-miner MaterialProperties ('Assessment of the properties of the deposited film'); Popov 2025 Sec. I ('properties of the deposited films, such as film thickness, morphology, conformality, and composition')]
    • growth holds 11 (quantity 11): How much film is deposited and how it builds up: thickness, growth per cycle, nucleation period, and thickness variation over a planar substrate. [Cremers 2019 Sec. I ('After each ALD cycle, a certain amount of material (coating) is deposited on the surface, which is called the growth per cycle'), Sec. II A (uniformity); schema-miner ThicknessControl and Uniformity]
      ids: growth_per_cycle, nucleation_period, thickness_nonuniformity, film_thickness, mass_gain, moles_deposited, moles_from_mass_gain, atoms_per_cycle, coating_mass_fraction, shell_volume_fraction, deposition_rate
    • conformality holds 6 (quantity 6): Measures of how far and how evenly the film reaches into a 3D feature: normalized thickness profile, penetration depth, coated aspect ratio, step coverage. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II H 'Ways to express the level of conformality' (step coverage, PD50%, PD80%, coated (E)AR); Tables II-IV column 'Coated EAR']
      ids: coated_aspect_ratio, penetration_depth, step_coverage, normalized_thickness, fill_fraction, front_slope
    • film_properties holds 18 (categorical 2, quantity 16): Optical, electrical, compositional and structural properties of the deposited film. [schema-miner MaterialProperties (OpticalProperties, ElectricalProperties, ChemicalComposition, Crystallinity, FilmDensity); Popov 2025 Sec. I ('properties of the deposited films')]
      ids: crystallinity, refractive_index, absorption_coefficient, resistivity, carrier_density, mobility, composition, film_density, roughness, binding_energy, crystallite_size, sheet_resistance, density_relative_to_bulk, phase, element_fraction, element_ratio, phase_fraction, h_per_metal
  • modelling holds 16 (quantity 10, model 6): The description of reactant transport and surface reaction used to simulate thickness profiles: the surface-reaction parameters and the models that use them. [Cremers 2019 Sec. V 'Simulation models on conformality of ALD', Sec. V C 4 'Reaction mechanism', Sec. V D 2 'Thickness profile and sticking coefficient']
    • surface_kinetics holds 8 (quantity 8): Parameters of the lumped Langmuir description of the surface reaction: sticking and recombination probabilities, adsorption equilibrium constant, saturation growth per cycle, surface coverage. [Cremers 2019 Sec. II E 'Reaction mechanisms and sticking probability' (Eqs. 5-9: s0, theta, k_ads and k_des; recombination probability r); Sec. V C 4]
      ids: initial_sticking_coefficient, recombination_probability, surface_coverage, adsorption_equilibrium_constant, saturation_growth_per_cycle, saturation_density, b_film, p_d
    • transport holds 2 (quantity 2): The gas transport regime inside the feature: the reactant's mean free path and its ratio to the feature size, the Knudsen number, which decides between free-molecular (Knudsen) and viscous flow. [Cremers 2019 Sec. V C 1 'Transport' (Knudsen diffusion in features whose size is below the mean free path; the Knudsen number); Sec. V A]
      ids: knudsen_number, mean_free_path
    • models holds 6 (model 6): Named transport-reaction models, analytical or computational, whose parameters are the surface_kinetics entries. [Cremers 2019 Sec. V A 'Overview' (1. Analytical models, 2. Computational models), Table VIII]
      ids: ylilammi_diffusion_reaction, gordon_plug_flow, yanguas_gil_markov_chain, arts_slope, elam_monte_carlo, heikkinen_porous_diffusion_reaction
  • reaction_monitoring holds 6 (quantity 6): In-situ gas-phase observations during the process: the pressure rise and its terminal slope in a closed chamber, the time to the pressure maximum, the moles of gas released and of solid deposited per cycle, and their ratio (the gas-to-solid stoichiometry). [gre23 (10.1116_6.0003182) Sec. III A, Eqs. 1-2, Table II: the pressure-trace analysis of static-dose ALD; no review passage groups these (Sec. 18.10)]
    ids: deposited_moles_per_cycle, gas_moles_per_cycle, reaction_stoichiometry, pressure_rise, pressure_rate, time_to_maximum
  • measurement holds 13 (quantity 13): Settings of the characterization and the signal axes it records: probe spot, objective magnification, a spectrum's abscissa; attributes of a measurement, not properties of the film or the process. [Cremers 2019 Table I column 'Characterization method' and Tables II-IV (the technique recorded per experiment); Sec. IV ('film thickness as a function of the depth')]
    ids: probe_resolution, objective_magnification, raman_shift, fit_r2, signal_intensity, diffraction_angle, molar_mass, molecule_diameter, reactant_number_density, wavelength_range, particle_radius, surface_to_pore_volume_ratio, tortuosity_over_porosity
